We see this situation, as well as this different picture in Latvian schools, especially in regions, as well as in the schools of the capital... How important this... this weekend, lunch availability, warm meal availability. I would really take an objective look at this issue. We would look at the studies that have been carried out in Europe, among other things in Germany, where it is clearly shown that regular, warm lunches, preferably from biological products, reduce hyperactivity for children, reduce child obesity, because they are less eating snout and drink soft markets - sweetened drinks - because they have regular hot lunches. The third point, which is very important, is that children are making better progress. When a child is fed, he has a high - quality, warm meal and improves his performance at school. So, colleagues, it's really... If we look at our children, then this principle is very important for Latvian municipalities to have the best possible and as many different circumstances as possible in order to be able to...

ParlaMint-LV_2021-11-22-PT13-2325-U390	The proposal provides for the phasing-in of premiums between 2022 and 2040 for old-age and invalidity pensions granted between 2012 and 2039, and thereafter for all recipients of old-age and invalidity pensions from 2040 onwards. More than eight years ago, for the purpose of saving social budget expenditure, a decision was taken no longer to grant a retirement pension to those who retired in 2012 and beyond. Those who were granted the right to retire by the end of 2011 shall receive, in addition to their pension,... a EUR premium. And so there was a dividing line: those who were entitled to a pension in December 2011 receive benefits, but those who are granted a pension after a month - do not receive them. Injustice was justified with a view to ensuring the sustainability of the special budget as the special budget fell in those years. Since so far the objective could serve as a special objective for the rescue of the budget, this injustice must now be eliminated as soon as possible from the point of view of justice. And the average benefit for pensioners would be about 20 euros per month if pension bonuses were renewed. Every euro in addition to the pensioner's wallet is spent and returned to the economy. Premiums have not lost their actuality and therefore... still a large proportion of seniors do not receive them, although much of their lives have worked during the Soviet period for which these premiums are calculated. At present, more than 350 000 pensioners receive pension benefits, while persons who do not receive pension benefits have more than 100 000 pensioners. In 2022, the additional funding needed for the gradual grant of the premium is almost seven million. And with each year, this funding is increasing slightly. In 2023, an additional 11.6 million will be needed, and in 2024 – 21.4 million. But... This is, in principle, our debt to the older men who need to be repaid, and this injustice must stop. Thank you.

ParlaMint-LV_2021-11-22-PT13-2325-U418	Let me explain how important a family assistant service is as a preventive social work. The Ministry of Welfare within the framework of the project “Professional Social Work Development in Municipalities” had started training for municipal family assistants. In total, the prospective family assistants involved in 100 hours and trainings from 37 Latvian municipalities will continue to provide the services of the family assistant after two years of training in the framework of the pilot project. The service of a family assistant is provided to a person or family whose ability to function and integrate into society due to various social, mental or physical impairments is difficult. And the service is an important support for clients in their place of residence, in a social environment, to promote the acquisition, strengthening of social skills, for example, how to take care of children, even as primitively as taking care of the child, to cook. And according to the collection of data by the Ministry currently provides services closer to the service of a family assistant in Latvia in 30 municipalities. And several more municipalities had expressed that wish. We are constantly fighting the consequences. But the consequences are much more expensive, and the results are much lower. This is why preventive work is very important, and the service of a family assistant is important for individual assistance in key areas of social work such as preventive work. It is work with the family to prevent the child from being taken out of the family, the parents from taking away the rights of the parents, and the child being handed over to the orphanage. As a bright example here, I would like to mention the Naukšens as these children arrived there. I had left for them, and half of them came from the orphanage. All of these children, talking about their parents, have a glow in their actions, talk about their parents in their hearts with warmth, and the fate of many would certainly be different if family problems and family support had been noticed in good time. It is a professional additional resource for social workers, especially where congestion of social workers, high burning risks and a large number of active customers have been identified. In the absence of the necessary funding, municipalities may also choose not to continue the service of a family assistant after the end of the project, and therefore all of this work will disappear in the absence. I therefore ask for support for this proposal.

That is why what is proposed this time by colleagues from the opposition is really very, very important. I'm just sorry to ask for money to build a ventilation system for only one regional hospital. I would ask for a separate ventilation system in sub-units to be in each of the regional hospitals, so that we can really then say that the country has done absolutely everything to make our people, who are delivered to sub-units, recover there, not die there. Please support me.

Today more and more children with special needs are studying in general education schools in Latvia. According to IBM, 67 percent of municipal schools in our country implement inclusive education.
